Return an opponent's character to hand ?=要將對手的 1 張角色返回手牌嗎？
Discard a card to return an opponent's character to hand ?=要捨棄 1 張卡，將對手的 1 張角色返回手牌嗎？
Choose an effect to perform once.\n1: You may discard 1 «Saga» character. If you do, return this card to your hand.\n2: You may choose a card in your level zone and put it in your waiting room. If you do, put this card in your level zone.\n3: Put this card in the waiting room.\n4: Put this card in your memory.=請選擇要執行 1 次的效果。\n1：你可以捨棄 1 張《Saga》角色。這麼做的話，將這張卡返回手牌。\n2：你可以選擇你等級區的 1 張卡放置到控制室。這麼做的話，將這張卡放置到你的等級區。\n3：將這張卡放置到控制室。\n4：將這張卡放置到你的回想區。
Choose up to 1 "Franchouchou" Sakura to put in memory.=請選擇最多 1 張「Franchouchou」Sakura，放置到回想區。
Choose a character to give +1000 power and the following ability. "[A] [(1) Discard 1 card] When this card's battle opponent becomes reverse, you may pay the cost. If you do, deal 3 damage to your opponent."=請選擇 1 張角色，給予戰力 +1000 和以下能力。「【自】[(1) 捨棄 1 張卡] 這張卡的戰鬥對手變成倒置時，你可以支付費用。支付的話，給予對手 3 點傷害。」
Choose a character to give the following effect. "[A] When this card's battle opponent becomes reverse, search your deck for up to 1 «Saga» character, reveal it, add it to your hand and shuffle your deck."=請選擇 1 張角色，給予以下效果。「【自】這張卡的戰鬥對手變成倒置時，從你的牌庫搜尋最多 1 張《Saga》角色，給對手看之後加入手牌，然後將牌庫洗牌。」
Choose an effect to perfrom.\n1: This card gets +2000 power until end of turn.\n2:You may choose a «Saga» character in your waiting room and return it to your hand. If you do, discard 1 card.=請選擇要執行的效果。\n1：直到回合結束為止，這張卡的戰力 +2000。\n2：你可以選擇控制室中的 1 張《Saga》角色，返回手牌。這麼做的話，捨棄 1 張卡。
Choose a climax card to not return to your deck.=請選擇不返回牌庫的名場面卡。
